Once an account is restored (deleted_at NULL), the un-expired `purpose:'restore'` JWT — explicitly rejected on EVERY REST route by the run-#31 replay guard (`jwt-cookie.strategy.ts:182-197`, "This restore token has already been used") — opens a full socket session for up to 7 days: chat, DMs, and the `ops` room if the account held a staff role. | P2-41 | API/Wallet | **Koralink-booking wallet TOCTOU — FIXED run #25 (8d890ce, Reviewer-A IMPORTANT #1)**: `createMatch` (koralink mode) read the host's `wallet_balance` with a plain SELECT inside the booking tx, then ran an UPDATE with NO `wallet_balance >= cost` guard. Two concurrent koralink match creations by the same host against two different slots could both pass the in-tx balance check, both subtract, and drive the balance negative. Same fix class as P0-4 (run #13) + P2-39 (run #22): the integrity guard is now INSIDE the row update (`UPDATE users SET wallet_balance = wallet_balance - $cost WHERE id=$hostId AND wallet_balance >= $cost`), zero rows = `BadRequestException` (re-reads the user for the same-shape error message), ledger insert is reached only on a successful deduct. 4 jest specs (sufficient balance, insufficient balance w/ no ledger, concurrent same-host two-slot race, self-mode unaffected). Pino warn on the zero-rows path carries `hostId/required/slotId`. **BUNDLED with Reviewer-A #3**: `notifications.service.ts:234-238` Sentry capture now fires only on `statusCode >= 500` or `statusCode === undefined` (timeout); 4xx per-subscription noise (429, 400) was flooding Sentry — now a `status_code` tag (filterable) + debug log only. **Gates**: jest 213/213 (+4), vitest 272/272, tsc 0, build 3/3, dist grep confirms. **Live E2E deferred to run #26** (requires partner-side slot generation on a fresh pitch; spec covers all 4 branches). | apps/api/src/modules/matches/matches.service.ts:1247-1280; matches.koralink-wallet.spec.ts (4/4); apps/api/src/modules/notifications/notifications.service.ts:234-238 | **DONE ✅** (run #25: 8d890ce; build 3/3, jest 213/213, vitest 272/272) | docs/plans/run25-wallet-koralink-booking-tx/ |

Test pins the Hijri regression (سبتمبر present, رمضان/ربيع/شعبان absent). ## Areas in scope (every run must assess all four)

- **PWA** (`apps/player-pwa`) — player experience, i18n, offline, realtime UX, 5 UX states
- **Admin console** (`apps/admin`) — HQ dashboard + partner portal, RBAC, moderation queues
- **API** (`apps/api`) — NestJS endpoints, contracts, realtime gateways, auth
- **DB / Infra** — Drizzle schema, migrations, indexes, deployment services, observability

## Status legend

- `TODO` — queued, not started
- `WIP` — a run is actively building it (see latest RUNS report)
- `IN-REVIEW` — built, awaiting next run's verification pass
- `DONE ✅` — verified by a subsequent run (build + tests + spot-check)
- `BLOCKED` — cannot proceed; blocker noted in `kanban/RUNS/` and STATE.json

## Defaults + 48h Veto (adopted 2026-10-04, per Opus delivery audit rec 2)

Rows tagged `default+48h-veto` carry a proposed default in their Status cell. The run that
proposes it posts the default in the run report; **if Abdullah does not veto within 48h, the
next run builds it as written.** A veto = single chat line ("veto P2-147, use X") — no form,
no meeting. OWNER-ONLY (never defaulted): PSP choice (P0-2), email provider keys (P1-41),
release promote merge, PDPL/PII stances, RBAC org design (P1-59), credential/account actions.

## Rules

1. A run may only mark an item `DONE` **after** `turbo run build` + `npx vitest run` are green for its slices.
2. The **next** run re-verifies previous `IN-REVIEW` items before trusting them (claims ≠ facts).
3. New findings always cite evidence (file:line, endpoint, failing flow) — no vibes-based items.
